- **scope-and-execute**: Composite skill — understand a problem area, plan the work, execute it, and ship it in one chained workflow. Chains adt-repo-intake or ecosystem-health (analysis) → context-pack (load context) → plan (phased plan) → dispatch or loop (execute) → ship (merge). Use when the task is "fix the X area" or "implement Y end-to-end" — the single command for analysis through merge.
